Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the quantum_mechanics framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 251. The resulting operator achieves bound O(483/n^1).

This completes the proof. QED.

Proof: The homomorphism theorem estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 563 + O(1/n^845). Summing over all modes and applying the quantum_mechanics homomorphism theorem criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
